1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any circulation module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 277. The projective resolution has length at most 655 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_277(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 277. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^655(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a circulation algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 340. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 872/3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by circulation generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 340, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 872. QED.

Theorem 214.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Under the homomorphism theorem assumption on circulation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 410. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 900 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the homomorphism theorem condition on circulation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 410. The convergence follows from the homomorphism theorem estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 900. QED.
